LINUX DO Harmony - Native ECH Experimental Package
Third-party licenses and attribution

Prepared: 2026-10-27
Target: aarch64-unknown-linux-ohos / arm64-v8a
Cargo.lock SHA-256: f7263421f2b2eb4b1c87a56bdbd1b94fe7bcb2cbaf2d56e21d6563af686444c8

SCOPE AND PROVENANCE

This bundle accompanies the native ECH experimental application. The ordinary
ArkTS-only application does not include the Rust ECH or C++ runtime libraries.
The application's own license is in the repository's LICENSE and NOTICE files.
Third-party software remains under its respective upstream terms below.

The Cargo package inventory was resolved from Cargo.lock with cargo metadata
--locked --offline and cargo tree --locked --offline --target
 aarch64-unknown-linux-ohos --edges normal,build. It covers 75 locked packages
(67 in the normal dependency closure, 8 additional build dependencies).
Build tools and procedural macros are included conservatively for source
reproduction; listing a package does not assert that all of its code is linked
into the HAP. Unselected packages for other platforms or unused optional
features are not asserted to be distributed by this target.

Actual license, copyright, copying and notice files were read from the installed
Cargo registry sources. AWS-LC's bundled LICENSE files, including fiat-crypto,
are included. SPDX expressions below are upstream metadata; they do not replace
or modify the original license texts or require choosing every OR alternative.

The libc++_shared.so attribution is taken from the installed OHOS Native SDK
26.0.0.105 native/llvm/NOTICE, reproduced in full because it contains the
LLVM, libc++, libc++abi and bundled third-party notices. Its additional toolchain
notices are retained conservatively and do not imply that all SDK tools ship in
the HAP. The SDK's arm64 runtime matches the corresponding experimental HAP
runtime byte-for-byte; libc++_shared.so SHA-256:
f69947af100b652e349ae51e4a4888bcc7a327db9085272d318176ef1782bf0f

Rust standard-library copyright notices from the installed rustc 1.97.1 (8bab26f4f 2026-07-14)
are included as rendered text from COPYRIGHT-library.html, together with its
Apache-2.0 and MIT license files. These notices include upstream third-party
attribution supplied with the standard library; inclusion does not assert that
all platform-specific standard-library code is linked into this target.

Except for omitting HTML markup in the Rust standard-library notice, upstream
text bodies below are reproduced without modification. Identical byte-for-byte
bodies are stored once and referenced by their full SHA-256 identifier. Source
labels are package-relative or SDK-relative, with no local filesystem paths.
This document does not sublicense third-party code under the application license.

# Community Data License Agreement - Permissive - Version 2.0

This is the Community Data License Agreement - Permissive, Version
2.0 (the "agreement"). Data Provider(s) and Data Recipient(s) agree
as follows:

## 1. Provision of the Data

1.1. A Data Recipient may use, modify, and share the Data made
available by Data Provider(s) under this agreement if that Data
Recipient follows the terms of this agreement.

1.2. This agreement does not impose any restriction on a Data
Recipient's use, modification, or sharing of any portions of the
Data that are in the public domain or that may be used, modified,
or shared under any other legal exception or limitation.

## 2. Conditions for Sharing Data

2.1. A Data Recipient may share Data, with or without modifications, so
long as the Data Recipient makes available the text of this agreement
with the shared Data.

## 3. No Restrictions on Results

3.1. This agreement does not impose any restriction or obligations
with respect to the use, modification, or sharing of Results.

## 4. No Warranty; Limitation of Liability

4.1. All Data Recipients receive the Data subject to the following
terms:

THE DATA IS PROVIDED ON AN "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT REPRESENTATIONS,
WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, EITHER EXPRESS OR IMPLIED
INCLUDING, WITHOUT LIMITATION, ANY WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF TITLE,
NON-INFRINGEMENT, MERCHANTABILITY OR FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.

NO DATA PROVIDER SHALL HAVE ANY LIABILITY FOR ANY DIRECT, INDIRECT,
INCIDENTAL, SPECIAL, EXEMPLARY, OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES (INCLUDING
WITHOUT LIMITATION LOST PROFITS), HOWEVER CAUSED AND ON ANY THEORY OF
LIABILITY, WHETHER IN CONTRACT, STRICT LIABILITY, OR TORT (INCLUDING
NEGLIGENCE OR OTHERWISE) ARISING IN ANY WAY OUT OF THE DATA OR RESULTS,
EVEN IF ADVISED OF THE POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGES.

## 5. Definitions

5.1. "Data" means the material received by a Data Recipient under
this agreement.

5.2. "Data Provider" means any person who is the source of Data
provided under this agreement and in reliance on a Data Recipient's
agreement to its terms.

5.3. "Data Recipient" means any person who receives Data directly
or indirectly from a Data Provider and agrees to the terms of this
agreement.

5.4. "Results" means any outcome obtained by computational analysis
of Data, including for example machine learning models and models'
insights.

   file.
